# Authorisation matrix (phase B)

One row per inbound state-changing message type or ingest path: who may say it, about
which object, the line that binds the signer's authority to that object, every transport
that can deliver it, and the test that fails if that line goes. Started 2026-09-26
(session 3, five rows); rebuilt whole in session 32 (2026-10-03), which closes phase B.

## How the rows were built

1. **Evidence (2026-09-26).** Nine enumeration passes, one per area, wrote
   `phase_b_evidence/authz_<area>.md`: every dispatch site, check, signer, transport and
   suspicion, about 275 sections. The suspicions became `candidate_findings.md` and were
   fixed by class (HOL-SEC-003..082), never moved into this table.
2. **Re-check (2026-10-02, HEAD `d16deefe`).** Ten read-only passes re-checked every
   section against the code of the day (`phase_b_recheck/`). Every FIXED held; the
   leftovers became HOL-SEC-083..117 (candidate class P) and accepted risks AR-16..AR-19.
3. **Rebuild (2026-10-03, HEAD `1bac467f`).** Six read-only passes rewrote every row from
   the re-check at the current code, each citation a `path:line` plus a verbatim quote of
   that line, and added the paths built since. A script checked every citation (the quote
   sits on its line, at least 15 characters) and every guard test name: 1,737 citations,
   0 wrong. Session 32's own fixes then landed (HOL-SEC-118..125), and the rows they touch
   were updated; the citations were re-anchored to the final tree by the same script.
4. **What the lead re-read by hand.** Every OPEN row of the rebuild (A-CH04, A-CH05,
   A-F7/A-T20, server_mls A-10 and A-17..A-22, relay 0.2 and B-12), each confirmed in code
   before it went to a fix, and ten FIXED rows sampled across eight areas (channel A-CH12,
   crdt B-08 and N-01, identity A-18, server_mls A-06, dm A-DM-12, files A-V6, relay A-06,
   new N-02, media A-MED-09): every one held. The other rows are agent-verified with
   mechanically checked citations, which is stated here rather than implied.

Paths: Rust relative to `rust/hollow_core/src/`, relay as `relay-uws/src/...`, Dart as
`lib/...`. Line numbers are the tree of session 32; the quotes find the line again later.

## Verdicts

`V` binding present and matching policy on every transport, no finding needed.
`FIXED HOL-SEC-nnn` a finding fixed it and the fix is in the cited lines. `GONE` the path
was deleted (the row says what replaced it). `ACCEPTED AR-nn` a decided residual
(`accepted_risks.md`). `DEFERRED` decided to wait for a named phase. `OPEN` still weaker
than policy, with its decided session. Policy (who SHOULD be allowed) is decided with
Vitalik, never delegated: the decisions are in tmp4.txt section 1 and the finding files.

## Still open after phase B

- `server_mls:A-01`: FIXED HOL-SEC-049, HOL-SEC-053, HOL-SEC-054, HOL-SEC-083, HOL-SEC-092; OPEN HOL-SEC-121 (session 33, xhigh)
- `transport:A-T10`: DEFERRED phase G (K3)
- `relay:0.2`: OPEN (session 33, decided 2026-10-03)
- `relay:A-22`: DEFERRED phase G (K3)
- `relay:A-24`: OPEN (session 33, decided 2026-10-03); DEFERRED phase G (K3)

`server_mls:A-01` carries HOL-SEC-121 (a member back from away answers a removed joiner's
old parked ask); the two relay rows are the `fwd:` room roster and 0x09 wakes from a socket
D1 hides; the DEFERRED rows wait for Apple's notification filtering entitlement (K3).

## Guard tests

A guard test fails when the binding is removed; most were proven by a mutation (break the
guard, watch the test fail, restore), scripted in the sessions' `tmp_*_mutate.py`. Session
32 added the missing ones the re-check listed: the relay's own handler (`test_relay_live`,
both builds of the release-day switches), the kill ack on the full node, the fetch PreKey
and meeting guards, the second block check after a roster binds, the forwarder PreKey
check, VC signals from outside a call, MLS join and kick and KeyPackage gates, the real
`stateless_check`, the data-channel CRDT entry, both backfill arms, the guest file header,
the recovery coordinator, ShardDelete and the shard temp names.

## Server state (CRDT) (`crdt`)

| Row | Message / path | Policy (who may say it, about what) | Binding | Transports | Verdict | Guard test(s) | Residual / notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| crdt:0.1 | Shared remote-op gate (was `admit_remote_op`, now `ServerState::ingest_remote`) | A remotely authored op counts only when its author's own key signed it for this server inside the clock bound, it is new, and its author is a current member allowed to make it at its own point in HLC order; the relayer never matters. | `crdt/fold.rs:172` `pub fn ingest_remote(&mut self, ops: &[CrdtOp]) -> Ingested {`; `crdt/fold.rs:190` `if let Err(reason) = self.stateless_check(op, now) {`; `crdt/fold.rs:196` `if self.op_log_dedup.contains(&key)`; `crdt/fold.rs:231` `if self.op_allowed(&op) {`; `crdt/fold.rs:305` `if self.op_allowed(&op) {`; `crdt/server_state.rs:1698` `let Some(role) = self.author_role(&op.author) else { return false };`; `node/swarm.rs:6743` `let ingested = state.ingest_remote(std::slice::from_ref(&op));`; `node/sync_handler.rs:3430` `let ingested = state.ingest_remote(std::slice::from_ref(&op));`; `crdt/sync.rs:162` `let ingested = state.ingest_remote(incoming_ops);` | carried Olm CrdtOpBroadcast (A-01), MLS CrdtOp (A-03), sync batch (A-05), WebRTC gossip op (A-01), op inside ServerJoinResolved (A-07) and inside MemberKickBroadcast (A-09): all reach ingest_remote; plaintext frame dropped (lane, 0.6); push fetch has no CRDT ingest | FIXED HOL-SEC-050, HOL-SEC-047; ACCEPTED AR-11 | crdt_forged_author_op_is_rejected_on_every_ingest_path, crdt_future_hlc_op_is_rejected_and_owner_can_still_rename, authz_ops_need_a_member_author_acting_by_its_own_id, authz_fold_state_does_not_depend_on_arrival_order, `ingest_rejects_another_servers_op`, `ingest_rejects_unsigned_op` | admit_remote_op survives only as a cfg(test) copy (server_state.rs 709), so the admit_remote_rejects_* unit tests guard verify_author but not stateless_check; the harness tests above guard the real path. The WrongServer branch of stateless_check (fold.rs 249) has no test. A demoted or removed admin backdating ops to before its demotion is AR-11 (applies to every authority arm). The CPU cost of rebuilds forced by out-of-order ops (design E R4) rides AR-01's flood measurement. Legacy anchor: 0.5. Session 32: the `admit_remote_*` tests drive the real `stateless_check` through `ingest_remote` now (renamed `ingest_*`), WrongServer included. |

`vault_gates_stay_wired` (every write arm checks before `store_shard`; both rebuild sites gather before and drop after), `a_failed_legacy_rebuild_keeps_the_copies_we_hold_for_others`, `the_holder_we_asked_wins_over_unasked_stores_of_a_shard` | New since d16deefe (section 2 follow-up 3d). Residuals written in HOL-SEC-117 (no AR entry): nothing is judged before the manifest arrives (`shard_bytes_refused` returns no refusal without one); legacy erasure manifests pin nothing, so a failed rebuild deletes every participating copy and a bad holder can use up the 3 automatic pulls; the first manifest for a content id still wins (A-V10); an unasked streamed ShardStore registered first still drops the asked holder's stream until the person retries (A-V1). |

## The relay (C++) (`relay`)

| Row | Message / path | Policy (who may say it, about what) | Binding | Transports | Verdict | Guard test(s) | Residual / notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| relay:0.1 | Relay principal = the authenticated socket peer id (`auth_hello`, `auth`) | A socket speaks only as the peer id its own key derives, proven by a v2 signature over this relay's domain, this socket's one-time nonce, its mode and its license digest. | `relay-uws/src/ws_handler.cpp:304` `std::string derived_peer_id = derive_peer_id(public_key);`; `relay-uws/src/ws_handler.cpp:305` `derived_peer_id != peer_id`; `relay-uws/src/ws_handler.cpp:318` `bool challenged = !data->auth_nonce.empty() && frame->nonce == data->auth_nonce;`; `relay-uws/src/ws_handler.cpp:319` `frame->domain != auth_domain(config.domain)`; `relay-uws/src/ws_handler.cpp:325` `signed_msg = auth_v2_message(frame->domain, frame->nonce, peer_id, timestamp, *mode, license_digest);`; `relay-uws/src/ws_handler.cpp:335` `data->auth_nonce.clear();`; `relay-uws/src/ws_handler.cpp:336` `if (!verify_ed25519(public_key, signature, signed_msg)) {` | WS auth of the full node, the push fetch node and the forwarder (all sign only v2 via connect_and_auth, ws_client.rs:969-972); v1 frames through the switch (ws_handler.cpp:321-322) | FIXED HOL-SEC-063 | `relay-uws/test/test_auth_frame.cpp` ("the signed v2 message matches the client's pinned vector", "guest and fetch at once is no mode"; helper only); `auth_v2_message_matches_the_relays_pinned_vector`, `test_auth_message_format`; handler refusals (derive, nonce, domain): none, no ws_handler test | Switch ACCEPT_AUTH_V1 = true (ws_handler.cpp:235, :321-322): until it flips on release day a 0.11 v1 frame (no nonce, no domain) captured by another relay replays here inside the 60 s skew. A v1 login keeps no door nonce (:329, :336), so it never proves a door. The relay still lets any key holder log in as its own id, a master id included: HOL-SEC-083 refuses a bare master on the client by decision (a relay-side refusal was considered and not done). |
